# X6 — no-support B-independent quadratic root: exact threshold and capacity census

2026-11-26. New scoped Driver mathematical reduction / exact finite computation, not native Review or accepted Result. Same frozen Port 10, 1935 directions, six BRC, positive (17t,B,22t,D), strict 3/2 near set plus legal optimum, 47 necessary support labels and 49 active components; use R237 level-3 germ bank (220 germs). Source e1aac4ac173c9f792108fdd7fe5212c0248374e9:research_notes/portable_direct/2026-11-26_r2_x6_r237_targeted_two_component_germ_refinement.md. R238 closes the one-necessary-support-factor root family under its inherited coarse coverage premises.

## Reduction
For a homogeneous nonzero degree-two Q without any necessary support factor, every nondegenerate germ on a required support has a nonzero eventual sign, by R238 divisibility. For a depth-six root with five linear descendants per branch, let A,B be the distinct labels occurring on the two strict sides. Then A∪B has size 47, |A|,|B|<=31, |A∩B|<=15, and each exclusive side contains at least 16 labels. These conditions also hold for any finite bank containing at least one nondegenerate germ per support. Dropping exact root zero points is a relaxation; no output correctness inferred from passing it.

Consider Q independent of B, i.e. Q=a t²+b tD+c D². In the positive chart t>0, set r=D/t>0. If Q/t² has no sign change in r>0, all nonzero germ signs coincide and one branch carries all 47 labels; impossible in five linear queries. If it has exactly one sign-changing positive zero, its strict germ sign is a single linear threshold up to reversal. The other factor has constant nonzero sign on the positive chart; repeated positive zero alone has constant sign away from zero. With no support factor and one sign change, the same finite relaxed problem is covered by a pair of thresholds with one extreme mask constant on the finite bank; the positive chart full binary observation is also a single linear threshold in the one-simple-positive-root case. If two distinct positive roots, Q is a nonzero constant times (D-r1*t)(D-r2*t). Thus all nonconstant strict germ root masks in this B-independent family are covered by threshold pairs, including single-threshold masks using a constant threshold mask, or by the all-one-side mask immediately failing capacity.

The first nonzero of denominator*D(a)-numerator*t(a) and the same at b is the exact germ sign for a threshold. The endpoint ratios with positive t,D are its only positive breakpoints; at a ray with t=D=0 the other endpoint decides, at t=0,D>0 sign is positive. Use every breakpoint except the 14 necessary threshold support slopes (already covered by R238), one positive rational mediant in each intervening interval, and one larger than the maximum. A pair of equal masks gives the all-one-side case, so only distinct masks need enumerate. Exact branch swaps quotient unordered masks. Capacity filter counts distinct tags, not number of germs. For a side with 31 labels, any five-deep linear tree must start with an active support query: both remaining four-deep branches have at most 15 labels each. Enumerate every support and reject if none leaves both counts <=15. This is a necessary one-step test; no full suffix result claimed.

All 8911 distinct nonconstant pair masks checked (the constant root fails immediately). The finite bank covers the entire stated B-independent class with no necessary support factor; 7216 pair masks fail initial capacity and 350 further fail saturated one-step support capacity, leaving 1345 masks for deeper suffix tests, not actual feasible trees. No cutoffs. General Q with B-dependence, later first joint observation, and unrestricted K6 remain open. The residual mask list is included verbatim for continuation; thresholds are representative numerator/denominator pairs, not exhaustive continuous coefficient cones.

## Reproduction (exact integer implementation)
```js
function census(data){
const gcd=(a,b)=>b?gcd(b,a%b):a<0n?-a:a,pr=v=>{let g=v.reduce(gcd,0n);return g?v.map(x=>x/g):v},sg=x=>x>0n?1:x<0n?-1:0,dot=(a,b)=>a.reduce((s,x,i)=>s+x*b[i],0n);let G=[],tags=data.map(()=>0n),rat=new Map(),comp=0;for(let k=0;k<data.length;k++){let d=data[k],parts=d.normal.join()==="51,2,-1"?[[["1","9","69"],["3","17","187"]],[["2","5","112"],["1","0","51"]]]:d.normal.join()==="17,-1,0"?[[["0","0","1"],["1","17","391"]],[["1","17","69"],["1","17","22"]]]:[d.endpoints];for(let [a,b] of parts){a=a.map(BigInt);b=b.map(BigInt);let seq=[a,b];for(let z=0;z<([2,29].includes(comp)?3:1);z++){let next=[seq[0]];for(let j=1;j<seq.length;j++)next.push(seq[j-1].map((x,i)=>x+seq[j][i]),seq[j]);seq=next}for(let v of seq)if(v[0]>0n&&v[2]>0n){let w=pr([v[2],v[0]]);rat.set(w.join(),w)}for(let j=1;j<seq.length;j++)for(let [x,y] of [[seq[j-1],seq[j]],[seq[j],seq[j-1]]]){let bit=1n<<BigInt(G.length);G.push([x,y,bit]);tags[k]|=bit}comp++}}
let R=[...rat.values()].sort((a,b)=>sg(a[0]*b[1]-b[0]*a[1]));let support=new Set(data.filter(d=>d.normal[1]==="0"&&BigInt(d.normal[0])*BigInt(d.normal[2])<0n).map(d=>pr([BigInt(d.normal[0]),-BigInt(d.normal[2])]).join()));let candidates=[];let prev=[0n,1n];for(let v of R){candidates.push(pr([prev[0]+v[0],prev[1]+v[1]]));if(!support.has(v.join()))candidates.push(v);prev=v}candidates.push([prev[0]+prev[1],prev[1]]);
function mask(f){let A=0n,B=0n;for(let [x,y,bit] of G){let s=sg(f(x))||sg(f(y));if(s>0)A|=bit;else if(s<0)B|=bit}return [A,B]}
const M=new Map();for(let v of candidates){let [a,b]=mask(x=>v[1]*x[2]-v[0]*x[0]);if((a|b)!==((1n<<BigInt(G.length))-1n))throw Error("zero germ");M.set(a+","+b,{a,b,v})}
const S=data.map(d=>{let n=d.normal.map(BigInt);return mask(x=>dot(n,x))});let Q=[...M.values()],roots=new Map();const c=s=>tags.reduce((n,t)=>n+(t&s?1:0),0);let all=0,pass=[];for(let i=0;i<Q.length;i++)for(let j=0;j<i;j++){let {a,b}=Q[i],{a:x,b:y}=Q[j],p=(a&x)|(b&y),q=(a&y)|(b&x);if(p>q)[p,q]=[q,p];let k=p+","+q;if(roots.has(k))continue;roots.set(k,1);all++;let cp=c(p),cq=c(q);if(cp<=31&&cq<=31)pass.push({p,q,labels:[cp,cq],thresholds:[Q[i].v.map(String),Q[j].v.map(String)]})}
let hist={},remaining=[],satChecks=0,satExcluded=0;for(let z of pass){let k=Math.max(...z.labels);hist[k]=(hist[k]??0)+1;let bad=false;for(let side of [z.p,z.q])if(c(side)===31){let found=false;for(let [a,b] of S){satChecks++;if(c(a&side)<=15&&c(b&side)<=15){found=true;break}}if(!found){bad=true;break}}if(bad)satExcluded++;else remaining.push(z)}
let rh={};for(let z of remaining){let k=Math.max(...z.labels);rh[k]=(rh[k]??0)+1}
return {summary:{germs:G.length,ratios:R.length,positiveSupportSlopes:support.size,thresholdRepresentatives:candidates.length,distinctThresholdMasks:Q.length,rootMasks:all,capacityPass:pass.length,hist,satChecks,satExcluded,remaining:remaining.length,remainingHistogram:rh},remaining:remaining.map(z=>({p:z.p.toString(16),q:z.q.toString(16),labels:z.labels,thresholds:z.thresholds}))}
}
```
